# Hyper Stack Public API Evidence Matrix

The public inventory comes from nightly rustdoc JSON, so it reflects compiler-visible exported items, re-exports, public fields, variants, trait items, and inherent methods. Evidence is a static correlation over test, benchmark, fuzz, and dispatch-trace syntax. `qualified-static` matches are stronger than name/receiver heuristics; neither substitutes for runtime coverage.

Summary evidence cells are `qualified / any-static`: the first number counts only `qualified-static` matches and the second also includes name/receiver heuristics.

| Crate | Public Items | Callables | Tests Q/A | Benchmarks Q/A | Fuzz Q/A | Trace Q/A | All Four Q/A |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `hypercurve` | 3303 | 2282 | 162 / 1038 | 67 / 748 | 42 / 366 | 12 / 202 | 7 / 122 |
| `hyperlattice` | 756 | 548 | 29 / 194 | 96 / 315 | 6 / 198 | 96 / 309 | 4 / 110 |
| `hyperlimit` | 943 | 389 | 17 / 92 | 6 / 108 | 9 / 85 | 6 / 113 | 4 / 44 |
| `hyperreal` | 561 | 320 | 63 / 200 | 67 / 204 | 14 / 85 | 64 / 205 | 9 / 64 |
| `hypersolve` | 2518 | 500 | 15 / 316 | 12 / 216 | 10 / 113 | 7 / 55 | 7 / 32 |

Percentages below use qualified evidence only.

- `hypercurve`: tests 7.1%, benchmarks 2.9%, fuzz 1.8%, trace 0.5%, all four 0.3%
- `hyperlattice`: tests 5.3%, benchmarks 17.5%, fuzz 1.1%, trace 17.5%, all four 0.7%
- `hyperlimit`: tests 4.4%, benchmarks 1.5%, fuzz 2.3%, trace 1.5%, all four 1.0%
- `hyperreal`: tests 19.7%, benchmarks 20.9%, fuzz 4.4%, trace 20.0%, all four 2.8%
- `hypersolve`: tests 3.0%, benchmarks 2.4%, fuzz 2.0%, trace 1.4%, all four 1.4%
